// ------------------------------------------------------------
// XCOM: Enemy Within - Show eight soldiers in squad select screen (LWR)
//
// - Requires "Long War Rebalance" to be installed
// - Requires "Ultrawide resolution fix" to be installed
// - Add two extra soldiers to the squad select screen
// - In ultrawide resolutions 8 squad members can be edited at once
// - Small fix for odd-numbered capacity showing a stale card
//
// Download "Long War Rebalance" mod: https://www.nexusmods.com/xcom/mods/686
// Download "Ultrawide resolution fix" mod: https://www.nexusmods.com/xcom/mods/892
// Install using PatcherGUI: https://www.nexusmods.com/xcom/mods/448
//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Changelog 1.0.0:
// - Initial release
// ------------------------------------------------------------

MOD_NAME = Show eight soldiers in squad select screen (LWR)
DESCRIPTION = Show eight soldiers in squad select screen, and allow editing 8 in ultrawide resolutions.
AUTHOR = Sparq


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Update LWR hangar display to show eight 3D models, and fix unloading.
//
// - Extend existing XGFacility_Barracks.DEMOAddNewSoldiers to show 8 positions.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

UPK_FILE = XComStrategyGame.upk
OBJECT = XGFacility_Barracks.DEMOAddNewSoldiers : AUTO

[REPLACEMENT_CODE]

// Original LWR mapping: 11, 9, 7, 5, 3, 1, 0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10.
// The eight-position window uses entries 2 through 9.
54 00 <.arrGenders> 2C 0C 16
0F 10 2C 00 00 <.arrGenders> 24 0B
0F 10 2C 01 00 <.arrGenders> 24 09
0F 10 2C 02 00 <.arrGenders> 24 07
0F 10 2C 03 00 <.arrGenders> 24 05
0F 10 2C 04 00 <.arrGenders> 24 03
0F 10 2C 05 00 <.arrGenders> 24 01
0F 10 2C 06 00 <.arrGenders> 24 00
0F 10 2C 07 00 <.arrGenders> 24 02
0F 10 2C 08 00 <.arrGenders> 24 04
0F 10 2C 09 00 <.arrGenders> 24 06
0F 10 2C 0A 00 <.arrGenders> 24 08
0F 10 2C 0B 00 <.arrGenders> 24 0A

// Preserve the original cycling command handling and sound.
07 [@play_sound] 97 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 2C F0 16
    0F 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 93 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 1D <%i 256> 16
    06 [@remember_offset]

[#play_sound]
1C <Engine.Actor.PlaySound> 20 <SoundUI.MenuSelectCue> 27 4A 4A 4A 4A 16

[#remember_offset]
07 [@restore_offset] 9B 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 2C 80 16
    0F 01 <@m_iHighestPsiRank> 00 <.iNumSoldiers>
    06 [@clear_begin]

[#restore_offset]
0F 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 01 <@m_iHighestPsiRank>

// Remove all assigned soldiers from the displayed lineup.
[#clear_begin]
0F 00 <.I> 25

[#clear_loop]
07 [@display_begin] 96 00 <.I> 36 19 19 1B <HANGAR> 16 [@] <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> 00 ( 01 <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> ) [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> ) 16

    0F 00 <.kSoldier> 10 00 <.I> 19 19 1B <HANGAR> 16 [@] <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> 00 ( 01 <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> ) [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> )

    07 [@clear_next] 77 00 <.kSoldier> 2A 16
        19 00 <.kSoldier> [@] <NullRef> 00 ( 1B <SetHQLocation> 25 4A 4A 4A 16 )

[#clear_next]
A5 00 <.I> 16
06 [@clear_loop]

// Display eight positions instead of six.
[#display_begin]
0F 00 <.I> 2C 02

[#display_loop]
07 [@done] 96 00 <.I> 2C 0A 16

    // Validate I + cycling offset before indexing arrGenders.
    07 [@display_next] 99 92 00 <.I> 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 16 25 16
    07 [@display_next] 96 92 00 <.I> 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 16 2C 0C 16

    // Validate the mapped dropship index.
    07 [@display_next] 99 38 3A 10 92 00 <.I> 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 16 00 <.arrGenders> 25 16

    07 [@display_next] 96 38 3A 10 92 00 <.I> 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 16 00 <.arrGenders> 36 19 19 1B <HANGAR> 16 [@] <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> 00 ( 01 <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> ) [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> ) 16

    0F 00 <.kSoldier> 10 38 3A 10 92 00 <.I> 00 <.iNumSoldiers> 16 00 <.arrGenders> 19 19 1B <HANGAR> 16 [@] <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> 00 ( 01 <XGFacility_Hangar.m_kSkyranger> ) [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> )

    07 [@display_next] 77 00 <.kSoldier> 2A 16

    // Original direct destination mapping; no special swap of slots 6/7.
    // Target order: 7, 5, 3, 1, 0, 2, 4, 6.
    19 00 <.kSoldier> [@] <NullRef> 00 ( 1B <SetHQLocation> 26 4A 38 3A 10 00 <.I> 00 <.arrGenders> 4A 16 )

[#display_next]
A5 00 <.I> 16
06 [@display_loop]

[#done]
04 0B
53


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Change logic to avoid maxing out 100k loops in one tick that causes UnreadEngine cut-off
//
// - Move compaction from UISquadSelect_SquadList.UnloadSoldier to XGChooseSquadUI.OnUnloadSoldier
// - XGChooseSquadUI.OnUnloadSoldier defers/groups all 'UpdateView' redraws to new tick using UpdateView timer (0.05s).
// ------------------------------------------------------------

OBJECT = UISquadSelect_SquadList.UnloadSoldier : AUTO
[REPLACEMENT_CODE]

07 [@done] 81 2D 01 <@m_bInSoldierView> 16

// Original tutorial restriction.
07 [@try_unload] 82 19 19 2E <Class.UISquadSelect> 01 <XComGame.UI_FxsPanel.screen> [@] <UISquadSelect.GetMgr.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetMgr> 16 ) [@] <XGStrategyActorNativeBase.ISCONTROLLED.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <ISCONTROLLED> 16 ) 18 [@] ( 96 19 19 19 2E <Class.UISquadSelect> 01 <XComGame.UI_FxsPanel.screen> [@] <UISquadSelect.GetMgr.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetMgr> 16 ) [@] <XGStrategyActorNativeBase.Game.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <Game> 16 ) [@] <XGStrategy.GetNumMissionsTaken.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetNumMissionsTaken> 4A 16 ) 2C 02 16 16 )
    04 0B

[#try_unload]
07 [@done] 19 19 2E <Class.UISquadSelect> 01 <XComGame.UI_FxsPanel.screen> [@] <UISquadSelect.GetMgr.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetMgr> 16 ) [@] <XGChooseSquadUI.OnUnloadSoldier.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <OnUnloadSoldier> 35 <UISquadSelect_SquadList.UISquadList_UnitBox.iIndex> <@UISquadList_UnitBox> 00 00 10 01 <@m_iCurrentSelection> 01 <@m_arrUIOptions> 35 <UISquadSelect_SquadList.UISquadList_UnitBox.iIndex> <@UISquadList_UnitBox> 00 00 10 01 <@m_iCurrentSelection> 01 <@m_arrUIOptions> 16 )

// Compaction is handled inside the manager, before these UI/data refreshes.
19 19 2E <Class.UISquadSelect> 01 <XComGame.UI_FxsPanel.screen> [@] <UISquadSelect.GetMgr.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetMgr> 16 ) [@] <NullRef> 00 ( 1B <UpdateSquad> 16 )
0F 01 <@m_iMaxSlots> 19 19 19 19 2E <Class.UISquadSelect> 01 <XComGame.UI_FxsPanel.screen> [@] <UISquadSelect.GetMgr.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetMgr> 16 ) [@] <XGChooseSquadUI.m_kMission> 00 ( 01 <XGChooseSquadUI.m_kMission> ) [@] <XGMission.GetAssignedSkyranger.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetAssignedSkyranger> 16 ) [@] <XGShip_Dropship.GetCapacity.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetCapacity> 16 )
19 2E <Class.UISquadSelect> 01 <XComGame.UI_FxsPanel.screen> [@] <NullRef> 00 ( 1B <UpdateButtonHelp> 16 )
1B <UpdateData> 16

[#done]
04 0B
53


// Manager: compact if capacity requires it, then schedule the 3D rebuild separately.
OBJECT = XGChooseSquadUI.OnUnloadSoldier : AUTO
[REPLACEMENT_CODE]

0F 00 <.kSkyranger> 19 01 <@m_kMission> [@] <XGMission.GetAssignedSkyranger.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetAssignedSkyranger> 16 )

07 [@failed] 77 00 <.kSkyranger> 2A 16
07 [@failed] 82 99 00 <.iSoldier> 25 16 18 [@] ( 96 00 <.iSoldier> 36 19 00 <.kSkyranger> [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> ) 16 16 )

// A valid array index may still contain an empty slot.
07 [@failed] 77 10 00 <.iSoldier> 19 00 <.kSkyranger> [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> ) 2A 16

// Preserve the original protected-soldier check.
07 [@unload] 9A 35 <XComGame.XGTacticalGameCoreNativeBase.TSoldier.iPsiRank> <XComGame.XGTacticalGameCoreNativeBase.TSoldier> 00 00 19 10 00 <.iSoldier> 19 00 <.kSkyranger> [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> ) [@] <XGStrategySoldier.m_kSoldier> 00 ( 01 <XGStrategySoldier.m_kSoldier> ) 2C 07 16
    1B <PlayBadSound> 16
    04 28

[#unload]
07 [@unload_succeeded] 81 19 1B <BARRACKS> 16 [@] <XGFacility_Barracks.UnloadSoldierFromSlot.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <UnloadSoldierFromSlot> 10 00 <.iSoldier> 19 00 <.kSkyranger> [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> ) 00 <.SlotIdx> 00 <.kSkyranger> 16 ) 16
    1B <PlayBadSound> 16
    04 28

[#unload_succeeded]
// Restore the original LWR trigger: capacity smaller than the array's slot extent.
// Run before refreshing loadouts/cards so they receive the new dropship indices.
07 [@refresh] 96 19 00 <.kSkyranger> [@] <XGShip_Dropship.GetCapacity.ReturnValue> 00 ( 1B <GetCapacity> 16 ) 36 19 00 <.kSkyranger> [@] <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> 00 ( 01 <XGShip_Dropship.m_arrSoldiers> ) 16
    19 1B <BARRACKS> 16 [@] <NullRef> 00 ( 1B <RemoveNoneSoldiersFromDropship> 00 <.kSkyranger> 16 )
    // SetTimer(0.05, false, 'UpdateView'): one-shot callback on this manager.
    // Uses Actor.SetTimer's indexed native, as used elsewhere in this package.
    // Re-setting the same named timer coalesces multiple pending requests.
    61 18 1E <%f 0.05> 28 21 <UpdateView> 4A 16

[#refresh]
// Always refresh data/launch eligibility immediately, but do not synchronously
// call UpdateView/DEMOAddNewSoldiers from this removal stack.
1B <UpdateSquad> 16
1B <UpdateLaunchButton> 16
04 27

[#failed]
04 28
04 3A <.ReturnValue>
53

// ------------------------------------------------------------

OBJECT = gfxSquadSelect.SquadSelect : AUTO
RESIZE = 140815
REL_OFFSET = 0
